<script type="text/javascript">
    (function () {
        var $popup = jQuery('#patient-info-popup');
        if (!$popup.length) { return; }

        jQuery(function () {
            jQuery("#PatientInfo_Medicare_NumberFirst").kendoMaskedTextBox({
                mask: "0000-00000-0"
            });
            jQuery("#PatientInfo_Medicare_NumberSecond").kendoMaskedTextBox({
                mask: "~",
                rules: { "~": /[1-9]/ }
            });
        });

        jQuery(document).ready(function () {
            jQuery("#patient-portal-access-tooltip").data("kendoTooltip");

            jQuery(document).on("click", ".js-toggle-section", function (e) {
                if (jQuery(e.target).hasClass("js-add-new-credit-card-for-new-patient") ||
                    jQuery(e.target).closest(".js-add-new-credit-card-for-new-patient").length) {
                    return;
                }
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                var target = jQuery("." + jQuery(this).data("target"));
                var arrow = jQuery(this).find(".js-section-arrow");
                target.slideToggle(200);
                arrow.toggleClass("collapsed");
            });
        });
    })();
</script>
